For these reasons, the Departments conclude that an in-person hearing is not required and that the written, supervisory review process established by the IFR provides respondents with a fair and meaningful opportunity to present their claims. Comment: One commenter also said that eliminating in-person hearings will put vulnerable populations at risk, such as aliens with low-income, a lack of representation, and a limited understanding of English. Citing Goldberg v. Kelly, the commenter alleged that because the respondents in this context are often low income, non-English speaking, and unrepresented by legal counsel, review of the paper file only is an insufficient means to afford due process. Another commenter also cites Goldberg v. Kelly, alleging that where DHS imposes such a toll on families and target “very means by which to live,” the private interests cannot be described as “modest.” Response: The Departments disagree that eliminating in-person hearings fails to protect vulnerable populations or violates due process principles, including those discussed in Goldberg v. Kelly, 397 U.S. 254 (1970). The Supreme Court has long recognized that procedural due process generally requires notice and an opportunity to be heard before the Government deprives an individual of a protected property interest, while also emphasizing that due process “has never been a term of fixed and invariable content,” FCC v. WJR, U.S. 265, 275 (1949), as its “exact boundaries are undefinable, and its content varies according to specific factual contexts,” Hannah v. Larche, 363 U.S. 420, 442 (1960). The circumstances presented here differ materially from those at issue in Goldberg. In Goldberg, the Court considered the termination of ongoing public assistance benefits that were essential to recipients’ subsistence and daily living expenses. The Court considered the deprivation at issue to be immediate and uniquely harmful, distinguishing it from “virtually anyone else whose governmental entitlements are ended.” Goldberg, 397 U.S. at 263–64. The Court therefore concluded that a pre- termination evidentiary hearing was required to minimize risk of an erroneous deprivation. Id.
相比之下，IFR所涉及的并非宪法财产利益的剥夺，而是依据法律授权对违反《移民与国籍法》（INA）第274D(a)(1)条、8 U.S.C. 第1324d(a)(1)条；INA第240B(d)(1)(A)至(B)条、8 U.S.C.
By contrast, the IFR concerns not the deprivation of a constitutional property interest but the imposition of civil monetary penalties authorized by statute for violations of INA 274D(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1324d(a)(1); INA 240B(d)(1)(A)–(B), 8 U.S.C.
第1229c(d)(1)(A)至(B)条；以及INA第275(b)(1)条、8 U.S.C. 第1325(b)(1)条所施加的民事罚款。这些罚款并不涉及维持生计福利或其他政府福利的终止。此类裁定通常也不取决于复杂的可信度评估，而是通常涉及书面证据和客观的法定标准。因此，政府出错的风险极小，且上诉程序提供了充分的纠错机会。相应地，Goldberg案中所涉及的程序性问题在本案中并未以相同程度体现。通常，民事罚款是在外国人已获得充分的正当程序机会以对针对其的裁定提出异议之后，并且在美国移民及海关执法局（ICE）和移民法官已就不遵守最终命令条款的后果向外国人作出充分警示之后才予以开具的。
1229c(d)(1)(A)–(B); and INA 275(b)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1325(b)(1). These penalties do not involve the termination of subsistence benefits or other government benefits. Nor do such determinations typically turn on complex credibility assessments; rather, they generally involve documentary evidence and objective statutory criteria. Therefore, the risk of government error is minimal, and the appeal process provides an adequate opportunity for error correction. Accordingly, the procedural concerns present in Goldberg are not implicated to the same degree here. Generally, civil fines are issued after aliens have received sufficient due process to challenge the findings against them and after the aliens have been adequately warned, by ICE and the immigration judge, of the consequences of failing to abide by the terms of the final order.
根据马修斯(Mathews)框架,正当程序要求程序设计应合理地确保公平性,同时兼顾所涉私人利益、错误剥夺的风险以及政府利益。将这些因素适用于本案证实,无需举行面对面听证。所涉私人利益虽然重要,但属于经济性质,而非维持生计层面的利益。错误风险已通过临时最终规则(IFR)多层次的程序保障措施得以缓解,这些保障措施包括:书面通知、仅需简短书面请求和说明的初步上诉、由监督移民官员进行的强制性重新审查(de novo review),以及该官员要求补充证据的权力,从而使外国人额外获得15天时间提供证据或说明。这些保障措施为外国人,包括新近抵达者、低收入者或英语能力有限者,提供了有意义的陈述机会。
Under the Mathews framework, due process requires procedures that are reasonably designed to ensure fairness in light of the private interest affected, the risk of erroneous deprivation, and the Government’s interest. Applying those factors here confirms that an in-person hearing is not required. The private interests at stake, while important, are financial in nature, not subsistence-level. The risk of error is mitigated by the IFR’s multi-layered procedural safeguards, including written notice, an initial appeal requiring only a brief written request and explanation, mandatory de novo review by a supervisory immigration officer, and the ability of that officer to request supplemental evidence, giving aliens an additional 15 days to provide evidence or explanation. These safeguards provide aliens, including those that are recent arrivals, low-income, or limited- English proficient, with meaningful opportunities to be heard.
各部门也不同意弱势群体需要面对面听证才能确保充分参与的观点。外国人可以寻求法律服务提供者或社区组织的协助。由于初步上诉的提交要求极低,不需要复杂的申报,外国人在最初15天期限内无需应对复杂的证据事项。在监督审查期间补充记录的能力进一步确保外国人能够澄清误解、纠正错误或提供补充信息,而无需正式的口头听证。
The Departments also disagree that vulnerable populations require an in-person hearing to ensure adequate participation. Aliens may seek assistance from legal service providers or community organizations. Because the initial appeal filing is minimal and does not require complex filing, aliens are not required to navigate technical evidentiary matters within the initial 15-day period. The ability to supplement the record during supervisory review further ensures that aliens can clarify misunderstandings, correct errors, or provide additional information without the need for a formal oral hearing.
6. 取消移民上诉委员会(BIA)审查 评论意见:多位评论者对临时最终规则(IFR)将上诉审查从移民上诉委员会(BIA)转移至国土安全部(DHS)的理由提出质疑,认为其理由不充分。这些评论者普遍认为,各部门错误地低估了这些处罚的法律复杂性,并断言国土安全部移民官员不具备裁决复杂法律争议所需的法律培训和经验。一位评论者还质疑临时最终规则(IFR)关于移民上诉委员会(BIA)缺乏裁决这些民事处罚经验的说法,指出临时最终规则本身就依赖移民上诉委员会的一项判例裁决来解释自愿离境处罚的范围。此外,评论者还表示,移民上诉委员会案件积压并非取消移民上诉委员会审查的正当理由。一位评论者表示,案件积压是司法部长(Attorney General)近期决定缩减移民上诉委员会规模所造成的自身问题。另一位评论者认为,移民上诉委员会的案件积压是国会拨款决定的结果,各部门不应试图通过取消移民上诉委员会对某些类别案件的管辖权来解决这一拨款问题。
6. Removal of BIA review Comment: Several commenters took issue with the IFR’s justification for moving appellate review from the BIA to DHS, suggesting that it was insufficient. These commenters broadly suggested that the Departments erroneously discounted the legal complexity of these penalties, asserting that DHS immigration officers do not have the legal training and experience necessary to adjudicate complex legal challenges. One commenter also challenged the IFR’s statement that the BIA lacks experience adjudicating these civil penalties, noting that the IFR itself relied on a BIA precedent decision to explain the scope of the voluntary departure penalty. Additionally, commenters also said that the BIA’s backlog of cases is not a valid reason to eliminate BIA review. One commenter said that the backlog is a self-created problem attributable to the Attorney General’s recent decision to reduce the size of the BIA. Another commenter argued that the BIA’s backlog is a result of Congressional funding decisions, and the Departments should not try to solve this funding problem by eliminating the BIA’s jurisdiction over certain categories of cases.
回应:首先,各部门不同意评论者关于各部门取消移民上诉委员会(BIA)对这些处罚的管辖权的解释不充分的说法。临时最终规则(IFR)已充分说明各部门取消移民上诉委员会审查的理由。这些理由包括:(1)这些处罚性质简单明确,且不存在需要移民上诉委员会裁决此类上诉的运作需求,例如既定的专业领域知识;(2)移民上诉委员会存在大量案件积压,上诉案件需要很长时间才能解决这一事实;(3)需要防止这些案件加剧移民上诉委员会的案件积压;以及(4)需要将移民及归化上诉执行办公室(EOIR)的资源集中用于其在《移民与国籍法》(INA)项下法定规定的职能,而民事处罚上诉并不属于该等职能范围。参见联邦公报90 FR 27441, 27444-47页。各部门认为上述理由合理成立,并建议读者参阅临时最终规则以获取进一步讨论。
Response: As an initial matter, the Departments disagree with the commenters’ suggestion that the Departments’ explanations for removing the BIA’s jurisdiction over these penalties are insufficient. The IFR fully explains the Departments’ reasons for eliminating BIA review. These reasons include: (1) the straightforward nature of these penalties and the lack of an operational need, such as an established subject-matter expertise, for the BIA to adjudicate such appeals; (2) the fact that the BIA has a large backlog of cases and appeals take a long time to resolve; (3) the need to prevent these cases from exacerbating the BIA’s backlog; and (4) the need to focus EOIR’s resources on its statutorily prescribed functions under the INA, which do not include civil penalty appeals. See 90 FR 27441, 27444–47. The Departments believe that these reasons are sound and refer readers to the IFR for further discussion.
各部门不同意这些处罚涉及复杂的法律问题,从而有必要进行移民上诉委员会(BIA)审查这一观点。各部门对评论者就临时最终规则(IFR)简化程序的合宪性、民事罚款金额、诉讼时效问题以及非法入境处罚范围所提出的论点并不认同,这些问题已在下文第III.D.8部分和第E部分予以说明。因此,评论者所举的例子对各部门得出的以下结论并无影响:(1)无需进行移民上诉委员会审查,因为这些民事罚款通常取决于直接的事实问题,监督移民官员已具备充分审查的能力;(2)不宜进行移民上诉委员会审查,因为移民上诉委员会有限的专门资源应更好地用于处理其前所未有的积压案件,而非审查监督移民官员已能充分审查的决定。参见联邦公报第90卷第27453页。此外,各部门指出,如果外国人认为监督移民官员作出的处罚决定在法律上存在错误,该外国人可以在用尽行政救济后,依据《行政程序法》寻求司法审查,或在为追缴罚款而提起的任何执法行动中对该决定提出抗辩。
The Departments disagree that these penalties involve complicated legal issues that warrant BIA review. The Departments are not persuaded by commenter arguments related to the constitutionality of the IFR’s streamlined procedures, civil penalty amounts, statute of limitations issues, and the scope of the unlawful entry penalty, which have been addressed below at Parts III.D.8, and E. Therefore, the commenters’ examples are inconsequential to the Departments’ conclusions that BIA review is (1) unnecessary because these civil penalties typically turn on straightforward issues of fact that supervisory immigration officers are prepared to adequately review and (2) inadvisable because the BIA’s limited, specialized resources are better used to address its unprecedented pending caseload rather than to review decisions that can be adequately reviewed by supervisory immigration officers. See 90 FR 27453. Further, the Departments note that if an alien believes that a supervisory immigration officer’s penalty decision was legally erroneous, the alien may, after exhausting administrative remedies, seek judicial review under the APA or may challenge the decision in connection with any enforcement action brought to collect the penalty.
关于一位评论者所称移民上诉委员会审查的价值体现在临时最终规则序言依据移民上诉委员会先例决定来解释自愿离境处罚范围这一主张,各部门指出,临时最终规则援引该决定仅为说明外国人一般而言对这些处罚可主张的抗辩理由有限。参见联邦公报第90卷第27443页。作为初步说明,所援引的决定,即Zmijewska案,并未涉及民事罚款问题,而是涉及外国人因未遵守自愿离境令而丧失移民救济资格的问题。24 I&N Dec. 87, 93-95(移民上诉委员会,2007年)。
Regarding one commenter’s claim that the value of BIA review is demonstrated by the fact that the IFR’s preamble relied on a BIA precedent decision to explain the scope of the voluntary departure penalty, the Departments note that the IFR cited that decision only to show that aliens generally will have limited defenses to these penalties. See 90 FR 27443. As a preliminary matter, the cited decision, Matter of Zmijewska, did not involve a civil penalty issue but instead addressed an alien’s ineligibility for immigration relief because of the alien’s failure to comply with a voluntary departure order. 24 I&N Dec. 87, 93–95 (BIA 2007).
即便如此,Zmijewska案明确指出,外国人因未能在获准自愿离境后离境而受到处罚的唯一例外情形“仅限于外国人并非出于自身过错而不知晓自愿离境令或因身体原因无法离境的情形”,这取决的并非复杂的法律问题,而是外国人可以轻易证明、并由监督移民官员裁决的事实问题。同上,第945页。因此,该移民上诉委员会决定并未削弱各部门取消移民上诉委员会审查的决定,反而切实支持了该决定。
Even still, Matter of Zmijewska clarifies that the sole exception to an alien being penalized for failure to depart after being granted voluntary departure is narrowly “limited to situations in which an alien, through no fault of his or her own, is unaware of the voluntary departure order or is physically unable to depart”—which turns not on complicated issues of law but on issues of fact that can easily be demonstrated by the alien and adjudicated by a supervisory immigration officer. Id. at 945. Therefore, this BIA decision does not undermine the Departments’ decision to remove BIA review but instead actively supports it.
此外,尽管移民上诉委员会完全有资格审理像这些民事罚款案件一样直接明了的案件,但其并未通过历史实践形成专门的专业知识,因为它仅审查过民事罚款案件,而从未在初审阶段裁决这些案件所涉及的事实认定问题。此外,正如临时最终规则正确指出的那样,移民上诉委员会即便在审查这些民事罚款的上诉案件方面,专门知识也十分有限。参见联邦公报第90卷第27450页(指出在特朗普总统第一任期内发出的民事罚款中,仅有少数上诉案件)另参见本序言第一部分(讨论在临时最终规则发布前数月内,移民上诉委员会受理的上诉案件数量相对于所发出的民事罚款数量而言十分有限)。正因如此,由移民上诉委员会而非国土安全部(DHS)内的监督移民官员来进行此类审查,实际上并无运作上的必要性,因为国土安全部才是在历史上实际负责裁决这些处罚并对此类裁定提供内部审查的行政机构,即便在移民上诉委员会拥有上诉管辖权的框架下也是如此。因此,各部门重申,移民上诉委员会在初审阶段缺乏裁决这些处罚的经验,且即便在审查这些处罚方面也缺乏切实的专业知识,这些都是取消移民上诉委员会上诉审查的正当理由(但并非唯一理由)。
Further, while the BIA is certainly qualified to adjudicate cases as straightforward as these civil penalties cases, it has not developed specialized expertise through historical practice, as it has only ever reviewed civil penalties cases, not adjudicated the factual findings they involve in the first instance. Moreover, as the IFR correctly points out, the BIA has little specialized expertise even in reviewing appeals of these civil penalties. See 90 FR 27450 (noting that only a handful of appeals resulted from civil penalties issued under President Trump’s first term); see also Section I of this preamble (discussing the small number of BIA appeals in proportion to the number of civil penalties issued in the months leading up to the IFR). Because of this, there is effectively no operational necessity to this review being performed by the BIA instead of by a supervisory immigration officer within DHS, the administrative body that has the historical practical experience of adjudicating these penalties and providing internal DHS review over such determinations, even under a framework where the BIA had appellate authority. Thus, the Departments reaffirm that the BIA’s lack of experience adjudicating these penalties in the first instance and lack of meaningful expertise even in reviewing them are valid bases (but not the sole bases) for removing BIA appellate review.
最后,两部门不同意移民上诉委员会(BIA)的积压案件并非撤销BIA对这些处罚决定审查权的正当理由这一观点。如上文所述,BIA的积压案件已促使有必要重新审视BIA作为上诉法庭的职能,并重新聚焦于实施简化案件处理程序的措施。因此,2026年2月,BIA发布了一项临时最终规则(IFR),除其他效率措施外,还确立了BIA一般将简易驳回上诉,除非其全体会议表决决定就案件实质内容进行审理。见91 FR 5267, 5270。47 剥夺BIA对国土安全部(DHS)实施和执行民事罚款及处罚事项的管辖权,该领域既无制定法要求移民法院执行办公室(EOIR)进行审查,BIA在此方面亦无独特的裁决专长,这将提高运作效率,因为BIA无需投入资源审理此类上诉案件,而此类案件数量可能随时间推移以及该临时最终规则旨在大规模实施此类处罚的简化流程而波动。
Finally, the Departments disagree that the BIA’s backlog is not a valid reason to remove BIA review of these penalty decisions. As discussed above, the BIA’s backlog has prompted a need for reconsideration of the BIA’s role as an appellate tribunal and renewed focus on implementing streamlining case processing measures. As such, in February 2026, the BIA issued an IFR establishing, among other efficiency measures, that the BIA will generally summarily dismiss appeals unless it votes en banc to consider the appeal on the merits. 91 FR 5267, 5270.47 Divesting the BIA responsibility over DHS imposition and enforcement of civil fines and penalties, an area where there is no statutory requirement for EOIR review and where the BIA has no unique adjudicatory expertise, creates operational efficiencies, as the BIA need not devote resources to adjudicate such appeals, which may fluctuate over time and with the IFR’s streamlined processes aimed at issuing such penalties at scale.
此外,司法部长近期决定减少担任BIA常任上诉移民法官的人数,并不削弱两部门关于保留BIA审查权可能不必要地延误处罚程序并加剧BIA积压案件的担忧。相反,正如EOIR在近期一项规则中所解释的,“扩大BIA规模并不必然意味着案件裁决数量相应增加或待审案件数量相应减少。”48 《缩减移民上诉委员会规模》,90 FR 15525, 15526(2025年4月14日)。EOIR还指出,BIA的规模已产生“重大的机构成本,包括47见Amica Ctr. for Immigrant Rts. 诉 EOIR案,案号CV 26-696(RDM),2026 WL 662494,第*34页(哥伦比亚特区地区法院,2026年3月8日)(部分撤销该规则)。
Additionally, the Attorney General’s recent decision to reduce the number of permanent Appellate Immigration Judges who sit on the BIA does not undermine the Departments’ concerns that preserving BIA review could unnecessarily delay the penalty process and exacerbate the BIA’s backlog. Rather, as EOIR explained in a recent rule, “increasing the size of the BIA has not necessarily equated to a corresponding increase in the number of cases adjudicated or a reduction in the pending caseload.”48 Reducing the Size of the Board of Immigration Appeals, 90 FR 15525, 15526 (Apr. 14, 2025). EOIR also noted that the size of the BIA has had “significant institutional costs, including 47 See Amica Ctr. for Immigrant Rts. v. EOIR, No. CV 26-696 (RDM), 2026 WL 662494, at *34 (D.D.C. Mar. 8, 2026) (partially vacating the rule).
48 正如EOIR在另一项近期规则中指出的,从2015财年到2025财年末,随着常任上诉移民法官人数的增加,BIA待审案件量增长超过五倍,从37,285件待审上诉增至202,946件待审上诉。《移民上诉委员会上诉程序》,91 FR 5267, 5270(2026年2月6日)(援引EOIR,《裁决统计:所有已提起、已结案及待审上诉案件》(2025年11月18日),https://www.justice.gov/eoir/media/1344986/dl?inline [https://perma.cc/88C5-MU4N])。
48 As EOIR noted in another recent rule, between fiscal year 2015 and the end of fiscal year 2025, as the number of permanent Appellate Immigration Judges increased, the BIA’s pending case load increased more than five-fold—from 37,285 pending appeals to 202,946 pending appeals. Appellate Procedures for the Board of Immigration Appeals, 91 FR 5267, 5270 (Feb. 6, 2026) (citing EOIR, Adjudication Statistics: All Appeals Filed, Completed, and Pending (Nov. 18, 2025), https://www.justice.gov/eoir/media/1344986/dl? inline [https://perma.cc/88C5-MU4N]).
对委员会决策过程的凝聚力及委员会成员间协作关系产生影响,其非先例性裁决丧失一致性,提供有效领导的难度增加,以及对委员会有限法律工作人员造成行政和监督方面的压力。”同上。这些缩减BIA规模的理由均未与两部门就撤销BIA对这些处罚事项管辖权所阐明的理由相矛盾,包括对BIA裁决时限及其积压案件的担忧。基于类似原因,两部门不同意必须等待国会为BIA提供额外资金,而不能撤销BIA对某些类别案件的管辖权这一观点。如该临时最终规则所述,国会并未要求EOIR或BIA审查这些民事处罚决定,《移民与国籍法》(INA)中也没有任何条款阻止司法部长行使其职权,撤销BIA对这些处罚事项的规章性上诉管辖权。90 FR 27448。
effects on the cohesiveness of the Board’s decision-making process and the collegiality among Board members, a loss of uniformity in its non-precedent decisions, increased difficulty providing effective leadership, as well as an administrative and supervisory strain on the Board’s limited legal staff.” Id. None of these reasons for reducing the size of the BIA contradict the Departments’ stated justification for removing the BIA’s jurisdiction over these penalties, including concerns about BIA adjudication timelines and its backlog. For similar reasons, the Departments do not agree that they must wait for Congress to provide additional funding for the BIA, rather than removing BIA jurisdiction over certain categories of cases. As discussed in the IFR, Congress has not required EOIR or the BIA to review these civil penalty decisions, and nothing in the INA precludes the Attorney General from exercising his authority to remove the BIA’s regulatory appellate authority over these penalties. 90 FR 27448.
评论:多位评论者主张,以国土安全部(DHS)上诉审查取代移民上诉委员会(BIA)的上诉审查,不当地将执法权力与裁决权力集中于一个机构之内。他们进一步主张,临时最终规则(IFR)的上诉结构造成了固有的利益冲突,因为国土安全部在审查自身行为时无法保持公正。一位评论者主张,这种将执法职能与裁决职能相结合的做法违反了正当程序原则。该评论者解释称,最高法院长期以来一直承认,正当程序要求裁决者保持公正性,任何可能产生有利于决策者的偏见行为的“可能诱因”的安排,均违反正当程序原则。参见Ward诉门罗维尔村案,409 U.S. 57, 60(1972年)。该评论者主张,由国土安全部评估这些罚款并审查上诉,至少产生了不当维持裁定的可能诱因,并指出该临时最终规则本身的颁布目的,就是为了让国土安全部能够更迅速、更大规模地实施这些罚款。另一位评论者指出,这些民事罚款被存入国土安全部的一个账户,该账户直接为移民拘留、遣返行动及边境墙建设提供资金。他们认为,这为国土安全部创造了一种强大的机构性动机,使其倾向于最大化罚款收入,因此,需要移民上诉委员会的审查来缓解这种潜在的利益冲突。为解决这些担忧,该评论者敦促两部恢复移民上诉委员会的审查,或设立一个独立于美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)和美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)的独立审查委员会,以审查民事罚款上诉案件。他们还建议,如果保留临时最终规则的结构,该规则至少应将罚款收入转入美国财政部普通基金,而非保留在国土安全部的执法账户中,从而切断国土安全部在征收这些罚款方面的财务利益。
Comment: Several commenters argued that replacing BIA appellate review with DHS appellate review impermissibly consolidates enforcement and adjudication powers within one agency. They further argued that the IFR’s appeal structure creates an inherent conflict of interest because DHS cannot be impartial when reviewing its own actions. One commenter argued that this consolidation of enforcement and adjudication functions violates due process. The commenter explained that the Supreme Court has long recognized that due process requires impartiality from adjudicators, and that any arrangement that gives rise to even a “possible temptation” to act in a biased way that benefits the decisionmaker violates due process. See Ward v. Village of Monroeville, 409 U.S. 57, 60 (1972). The commenter asserted that DHS assessing these penalties and reviewing appeals raises at least a possibility of temptation to impermissibly affirm the determinations, noting that the IFR itself was issued to allow DHS to impose these penalties more swiftly and at scale. Another commenter noted that these civil penalties are deposited into a DHS account that directly funds immigration detention, removal operations, and border wall construction. They suggested that this creates a powerful institutional incentive for DHS to maximize fine revenue, and, therefore, BIA review is needed to mitigate this potential conflict of interest. To address these concerns, this commenter urged the Departments to restore BIA review or to create an independent review board, separate from ICE and CBP, to review civil penalty appeals. They also suggested that if the IFR’s structure is preserved, the rule should, at minimum, sever the financial interest DHS has in imposing these fines by redirecting civil penalty revenue to the Department of the Treasury’s general fund rather than retaining it in DHS’s enforcement accounts.
回应:国土安全部坚决驳斥评论者关于国土安全部移民官员在裁决民事罚款上诉时无法保持公正和中立的推测性说法。国土安全部已设计了上诉程序,以确保外国人的上诉能够仅基于案情得到公正、充分的审议。事实上,临时最终规则明确规定,任何对罚款决定的上诉,都必须由未参与最初民事罚款决定的另一位监督移民官员进行审查。此外,临时最终规则指示该监督官员以重新审理的方式(de novo)审查上诉记录,从而确保官员仅依据所呈现的法律和事实对每项上诉作出裁决,而不对最初决定给予任何偏向性权重。政府机构的行为享有正当性推定,参见美国邮政总局案,534 U.S. at 10,而评论者并未提供任何支持其主张的证据,即移民官员仅因受雇于签发并执行这些罚款的同一机构,就会驳回原本有理的罚款异议。
Response: DHS strongly rejects commenters’ speculative suggestion that DHS immigration officers cannot be impartial and neutral when adjudicating civil penalty appeals. DHS has designed the appeal process to ensure that an alien’s appeal is fairly and fully considered on the merits alone. Indeed, the IFR makes clear that any appeal of a penalty decision must be reviewed by a separate supervisory immigration officer who was not involved in the initial civil monetary penalty decision. Additionally, the IFR instructs the supervisory officer to review the appeal record de novo, which ensures that officers decide each appeal solely on the law and facts presented without providing any weight in favor of the initial decision. There is a presumption of regularity that attaches to the actions of government agencies, see U.S. Postal Serv., 534 U.S. at 10, and commenters have offered no support for their claim that immigration officers will deny otherwise meritorious challenges to civil penalties simply because they are employed by the same agency that issues and enforces these penalties.
此外,最高法院长期以来一直认为,将执法职能与裁决职能合并于单一机构之内,并不违反正当程序原则。参见Withrow诉Larkin案,421 U.S. 35, 56至58页(1975年)(认定行政程序中的初始指控由随后裁决该事项的同一机构提出这一事实本身,并不违反正当程序原则);另参见Marcello诉Bonds案,349 U.S. 302
Moreover, the Supreme Court has long held that it does not violate due process to combine enforcement and adjudication functions within a single agency. See Withrow v. Larkin, 421 U.S. 35, 56–58 (1975) (holding that the fact that the initial charge in an administrative proceeding is brought by the same agency that later adjudicates the matter is not, in and of itself, violative of due process); see also Marcello v. Bonds, 349 U.S. 302
(1955年)(驳回了这一论点，即前移民归化局根据《移民与国籍法》先前版本将检控职能与裁决职能混同，剥夺了外国人在驱逐出境程序中的正当程序权利)。因此，各部不认同评论意见提出的观点，即在同一机构内整合执法职能与裁决职能违反正当程序或本质上增加了裁决偏颇的风险。一位评论者引用的判例法并不能削弱各部关于临时最终规则（IFR）上诉程序符合正当程序要求的结论。这些被引用的案例至多说明这样一项原则：如果裁决者在案件结果中具有直接的经济或金钱利益、已对案件存有预断，或存在其他具体的利益冲突，该机构的裁决程序可能违反正当程序。然而，这些案例并不支持该评论者提出的宽泛论点，即国土安全部（DHS）移民官员仅因隶属于国土安全部，就不能保持中立和不偏不倚。相反，判例法明确指出：即使行使调查职能与裁决职能相结合的行政官员，也应被推定具有诚实和正直的品格，必须提出实际存在偏见的证据，方可推翻这一推定。参见Marcello案，349 U.S. 第311页；Withrow案，421 U.S. 第47页；另见Baran诉博蒙特港口航运区案，57 F.3d 436, 446页（第五巡回上诉法院，1995年）。该评论者并未提出任何实际且非推测性的理由，以说明国土安全部官员为何不能保持中立，反而会被“诱使”违规维持其他国土安全部官员作出的处罚决定。
(1955) (rejecting the argument that the former INS’s comingled prosecutorial and adjudicative functions, under a prior version of the INA, deprived aliens of due process in deportation proceedings). Thus, the Departments disagree with commenters’ suggestion that the consolidation of enforcement and adjudication functions within the same agency violates due process or inherently increases the risk of biased decision-making. The caselaw cited by one commenter does not undermine the Departments’ conclusion that the IFR’s appeal procedures comport with due process. At most, the cited cases stand for the principle that an agency’s adjudicatory procedures could violate due process if the adjudicator has a direct financial or pecuniary interest in the outcome of the case, has prejudged the case, or has some other concrete conflict of interest. These cases, however, do not support the commenter’s broad argument that DHS immigration officers cannot be neutral and unbiased merely because they are part of DHS. Rather the caselaw is clear: there is a presumption of honesty and integrity in administrative officials exercising adjudicative functions, even where there is a combination of investigative and adjudicative functions, and some evidence of actual bias must be presented to overcome this presumption. See Marcello, 349 U.S. at 311; Withrow, 421 U.S. at 47; see also Baran v. Port of Beaumont Navigation District, 57 F.3d 436, 446 (5th Cir. 1995). The commenter has not offered any actual and non-speculative reason to think that DHS officers cannot be neutral but will instead be “tempted” to impermissibly affirm penalty decisions issued by other DHS officers.
评论者提出的其他担忧并不能支持其主张，即民事罚款上诉必须由移民上诉委员会（BIA）或国土安全部以外的机构裁决。各部承认，《移民与国籍法》第280(b)条，即8 U.S.C. 1330(b)规定，从外国人处收取的任何民事罚款应存入国土安全部的一个账户，用于资助移民拘留、遣返行动及边境墙建设。然而，国土安全部不认同一位评论者的观点，即认为裁决民事罚款上诉的国土安全部官员将被鼓励为该国土安全部基金最大化罚款收入。裁决民事罚款上诉的国土安全部官员须遵守严格的法律和程序标准，以确保其决策过程的公正性与公平性。49 这些官员的职责是依法适用法律并独立评估每个案件的事实，而不考虑其决定在财务方面的影响。50 国土安全部已建立健全的监督审查机制，以确保裁决工作符合适用的法律、法规及道德标准。罚款金额受制定法和法规约束，一旦移民官员确定应处以民事罚款，该官员在选择罚款金额方面的裁量权即受这些制定法和法规限制的约束，本规则并不改变制定法所授权的罚款金额本身。此外，《移民与国籍法》下民事罚款的主要目的是促使遵守移民法律，而非创造收入。国土安全部仍致力于维护裁决程序的公正性，确保所作决定仅基于每个案件的是非曲直，而不考虑与资金相关的任何外部因素。
The other concerns cited by commenters do not support their contention that civil penalty appeals must be adjudicated by the BIA or an agency outside of DHS. The Departments acknowledge that INA 280(b), 8 U.S.C. 1330(b), provides that any civil monetary penalties collected from aliens shall be deposited into a DHS account that funds immigration detention, removal operations, and border wall construction. DHS disagrees, however, with one commenter’s view that DHS officers adjudicating civil penalty appeals will be encouraged to maximize fine revenue for this DHS fund. DHS officers adjudicating civil penalty appeals are bound by strict legal and procedural standards to ensure impartiality and fairness in their decision-making processes.49 These officers are tasked with applying the law and evaluating the facts of each case independently, without regard to the financial implications of their decisions.50 DHS has robust supervisory review mechanisms in place to ensure that adjudications are conducted in compliance with applicable laws, regulations, and ethical standards. The fine amounts are governed by statute and regulations and, once an immigration officer determines that a civil penalty should be issued, the officer’s discretion in choosing the fine amount is bound by those statutory and regulatory limits, and this rule does not alter the penalty amounts themselves authorized by statute. Furthermore, the primary purpose of civil monetary penalties under the INA is to enforce compliance with immigration laws, not to generate revenue. DHS remains committed to upholding the integrity of the adjudication process and ensuring that decisions are made based solely on the merits of each case, rather than any external considerations related to funding.
同样，国土安全部不认同这样的观点，即由于临时最终规则序言强调国土安全部希望最大限度地运用这些罚款，官员将因此产生在上诉中维持处罚决定的动机。各部认同，该临时最终规则旨在使国土安全部能够按照在美非法外国人规模的比例，最大限度地实施民事罚款，而非追求罚款总额的最大化。该临时最终规则通过简化处罚程序实现这一目的，但并未规定任何处罚上诉的结果，也未以其他方式对移民官员施加压力，迫使其维持初始处罚决定。相反，如上文所述，该临时最终规则明确规定，必须由一名主管官员进行审查。49 例如，Goldberg诉Kelly案，397 U.S. 254, 271页（1970年）（认定负责裁决涉及财产事项的政府决策者必须保持公正）。
Similarly, DHS disagrees that officers will have an incentive to affirm penalty decisions on appeal because the IFR’s preamble emphasizes DHS’s desire to maximize the use of these penalties. The Departments agree that the IFR was designed to maximize DHS’s ability to impose civil penalties in proportion to the scale of illegal aliens in the United States—not the total amount of the penalties. The IFR achieves this purpose by streamlining the penalty process, but it does not dictate the outcome of any penalty appeal or otherwise pressure immigration officers to affirm initial penalty decisions. Rather, as discussed above, the IFR makes clear that a supervisory officer must review 49 E.g., Goldberg v. Kelly, 397 U.S. 254, 271 (1970) (recognizing that government decisionmakers adjudicating matters involving property must be impartial).
50 同上。
50 Id.
上诉完全基于国土安全部(DHS)的记录以及外国人提交的任何证据或论点进行重新审查。此外,该临时最终规则(IFR)为官员提供了45天的时间来裁决上诉,这将确保他们有充足的时间考虑外国人提供的所有证据和论点。因此,DHS认为,该临时最终规则序言中的声明不会导致移民官员对上诉先入为主或不当地维持处罚决定。DHS澄清,其期望每位移民监督官员仅根据法律和所呈现的事实来决定个案上诉。
appeals de novo based solely on DHS records and any evidence or argument submitted by the alien. Moreover, the IFR provides officers with a 45-day period to adjudicate appeals, which will ensure that they have ample time to consider all of the evidence and arguments provided by the alien. Accordingly, DHS believes that statements in the IFR’s preamble will not cause immigration officers to prejudge appeals or impermissibly affirm penalty decisions. DHS clarifies that it expects each supervisory immigration officer to decide individual appeals based solely on the law and the facts presented.
最后,各部门指出,确保移民官员遵守这些监管要求并根据每个上诉自身的事实和情况作出决定,并不需要移民上诉委员会(BIA)的复审。如果外国人认为移民官员的决定有误,且并非基于适用法律和案件事实作出,则在用尽行政救济后,该外国人可根据《行政程序法》(APA)寻求司法审查,或在司法部(DOJ)提起的追缴罚款的任何执法行动中对该决定提出质疑。
Finally, the Departments note that BIA review is not needed to ensure that immigration officers comply with these regulatory requirements and decide each appeal on the basis of its own facts and circumstances. If an alien believes that an immigration officer’s decision was erroneous and not based on the applicable law and the facts of the case, then after exhausting administrative remedies, the alien may seek judicial review under the APA or may challenge the decision in connection with any enforcement action brought by DOJ to collect the penalty.
基于上述所有理由,无论单独还是综合考量,各部门拒绝修改该临时最终规则以恢复移民上诉委员会的复审。同样,DHS拒绝设立一个独立于移民及海关执法局(ICE)和海关及边境保护局(CBP)之外的独立机构来裁决民事罚款上诉,因为DHS认为ICE和CBP的监督官员完全有能力在不带偏见或先入为主的情况下,根据每个上诉各自的事实和情况作出裁决。此外,设立独立委员会或机构将与该临时最终规则简化民事罚款程序(包括上诉程序)的宗旨相冲突,因为这可能需要额外的时间来创建并落实相关程序,以确保该委员会能够及时收到申请材料和记录。最后,关于一位评论者提出的主张,即为消除任何利益冲突,DHS应将罚款收入转入财政部一般基金,而非保留在DHS账户中,DHS指出,其不能违反《移民与国籍法》(INA)第280(b)条,即8 U.S.C. 1330(b)条的规定来转移资金。评论意见:多位评论者认为该临时最终规则的上诉程序缺乏透明度和问责制。他们指出,移民上诉委员会公开其决定,并拥有成熟的程序来公布新出现的法律问题的裁决。相比之下,评论者指出,该临时最终规则并不要求DHS公开其民事罚款决定,或在涉及类似问题的案件中将其视为先例。评论者认为,这将导致决定的随意性和不一致性,并造成问责缺失。
For all of these reasons, individually and collectively, the Departments decline to modify the IFR to restore BIA review. Similarly, DHS declines to create an independent body, separate from ICE and CBP, to adjudicate civil penalty appeals because DHS believes that ICE and CBP supervisory officers are fully capable of deciding each appeal on the basis of its individual facts and circumstances without bias or prejudgment. Additionally, creating an independent board or body would conflict with the purpose of the IFR to streamline the civil penalty process, including the appeal process, because it could take additional time to create and to put in place processes that would ensure that the board receives filings and records in a timely manner. Finally, with respect to one commenter’s claim that, in order to remove any conflict of interest, DHS should redirect penalty revenue to a general Treasury fund rather than retaining it in DHS accounts, DHS notes that it cannot redirect funds in contravention of INA 280(b), 8 U.S.C. 1330(b). Comment: Several commenters characterized the IFR’s appeal process as lacking transparency and accountability. They noted that the BIA makes its decisions publicly available and has a well-established process for publishing decisions on emerging legal issues. In contrast, commenters noted that the IFR does not require DHS to make its civil fine decisions public or treat them as precedential in cases raising similar issues. Commenters argued that this will contribute to arbitrary and inconsistent decisions and a lack of accountability.
回应:正如该临时最终规则及上文所述,本规则所涉及的民事处罚通常取决于简单明了的事实问题,而非复杂的法律问题。参见90 FR 27453。因此,各部门不同意评论者提出的关于DHS需要建立一个程序来公布涉及这些处罚的新出现法律问题裁决的观点。DHS还指出,ICE和CBP已就这些处罚向移民官员发布了指导意见,其中包括针对评论者所提出且本序言通篇讨论的各种法律问题的指导。最后,本规则中的任何内容均不妨碍外国人就处罚决定向联邦法院提起诉讼,也不妨碍其在司法部为追缴罚款而采取的执法行动中提出其可能适用的任何法律抗辩。DHS将遵守任何不利的法院裁决,并将适用因此类诉讼而产生的、适用于这些处罚的任何法律先例。
Response: As discussed in the IFR and above, the civil penalties covered by this rule typically turn on straightforward issues of fact, rather than complicated questions of law. See 90 FR 27453. Accordingly, the Departments disagree with commenters that there is a need for DHS to establish a process for publishing decisions on emerging legal issues involving these penalties. DHS also notes that ICE and CBP have issued guidance to immigration officers regarding these penalties, including guidance on the various legal issues that commenters have identified and that are discussed throughout this preamble. Finally, nothing in this rule prevents an alien from bringing a federal court challenge to a penalty decision or from raising any legal defense that may be available to the alien in connection with a DOJ enforcement action to collect the penalty. DHS will comply with any adverse court ruling and will apply any legal precedent applicable to these penalties that results from such a challenge.
就评论者所暗示的DHS必须公布民事处罚决定以在所评估的处罚金额方面提供透明度、问责制和一致性而言,ICE和CBP正在采取统一做法,即在符合诉讼时效的前提下,对每个案件评估法律授权的最高处罚金额。DHS有意迅速作出这些处罚决定并采用法律授权的最高金额,以促使外国人遵守遣返令和自愿离境令。为实现这两个目标,DHS已确定ICE和CBP应评估法律授权的最高处罚金额。
To the extent that commenters are suggesting that DHS must publish civil penalty decisions to provide transparency, accountability, and consistency in the penalty amounts being assessed, ICE and CBP are applying a uniform practice of assessing the maximum statutorily authorized penalty amount in each case, consistent with the statute of limitations. DHS has an interest in issuing these penalties quickly and at the maximum authorized amount to encourage aliens to comply with removal and voluntary departure orders. To achieve these dual objectives, DHS has determined that ICE and CBP should assess the maximum penalties authorized by law.
7. 减轻因素;重新开庭动议 评论意见:有评论者认为,该规则应允许外国人寻求减轻处理,并应明确国土安全部(DHS)在评估处罚时应考虑减轻因素。一位评论者建议采用基于违法严重程度和善意合规努力的分级处罚制度。另一位评论者建议各机构采用考虑接受处罚方经济能力的、基于经济状况审查的罚款结构。另一位评论者指出,政府对受处罚的雇主会考虑减轻因素。该评论者称,根据《移民改革与控制法》(IRCA)对雇用外国人的雇主评估罚款时,政府会考虑诸如企业规模、善意程度、违法行为的严重性、雇员是否为外国人,以及雇主以往违法记录等因素。最后,有一位评论者认为,该规则取消了外国人请求重新开庭的任何权利,同时却保留了国土安全部重新开庭的权力,这破坏了程序公正性。
7. Mitigation factors; motions to reopen Comment: Commenters argued that the rule should allow aliens to seek mitigation, and it should clarify that DHS should consider mitigating factors when assessing penalties. One commenter recommended a tiered penalty system based on severity and good faith compliance efforts. Another commenter recommended that the agencies adopt a means-tested fine structure that considers the recipient’s financial capacity. Another commenter points out that the government considers mitigating factors for employers subject to penalties. They state that in assessing fines against employers of aliens under the Immigration Reform and Control Act, the government considers factors such as the size of their business, their good faith, the seriousness of the violations, whether the employee is an alien, and the employer’s history of previous violations. Finally, one commenter argues that the rule undermines procedural fairness by eliminating any right for aliens to request reopening while preserving reopening authority for DHS.
回应:各部门不同意临时最终规则(IFR)应授权外国人寻求减轻处罚金额、建立分级处罚结构,或采用基于经济状况审查的处罚评估框架。本规则所涉及的民事罚款依据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第240B(d)条、第274D条和第275(b)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1229c(d)条、第1324d条和第1325(b)条。这些法定条款并未要求国土安全部或司法部(DOJ)根据减轻情节、经济能力或主观善意因素调整处罚金额,也未提供外国人请求酌情减轻处罚的途径。相比之下,评论者所引用的若干法规,包括《移民改革与控制法》下管理某些雇主制裁的相关条款,明确规定了国土安全部或司法部在评估处罚时必须考虑的减轻因素。国会在本案所涉的法定条款中并未纳入此类规定。此外,特朗普总统的行政命令指示国土安全部“确保对依法有权评估和征收的、针对非法居留在美国境内的外国人的所有罚款和处罚进行评估和征收”51,并“采取一切适当行动确保我国边境安全……包括通过威慑和防止非法外国人进入美国”52。与此同时,本临时最终规则并未排除国土安全部基于自身动议撤销或重新开庭有关决定的可能性,只要这样做对确保行政准确性、纠正错误或处理其他与健全执法相关的考量是必要的。这种内部权力属于程序性质,服务于该机构在一致性、公正性和纠错方面的利益。允许外国人在国土安全部提出重新开庭动议以寻求减轻处罚,特别是在监督审查程序之后提出此类动议,将损害该部门在终局性、行政效率和及时启动追缴程序方面的正当利益,而这些利益对于管理全国性处罚体系至关重要。本规则所涉及的依据《移民与国籍法》第240B(d)条、第274(D)条和第275(b)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1229c(d)条、第1324d条和第1325(b)条所产生的民事罚款,并未赋予外国人请求重新开庭最终民事罚款决定的法定权利。因此,各部门拒绝建立一种新的监管机制,使外国人实际上获得在监督审查结束后重新开庭最终决定或寻求减轻处罚的权利。
Response: The Departments disagree that the IFR should authorize aliens to seek mitigation of penalty amounts, create a tiered penalty structure, or adopt a means-tested framework for assessing penalties. The civil monetary penalties addressed in this rule arise under INA 240B(d), 274D, and 275(b), 8 U.S.C. 1229c(d), 1324d, and 1325(b). These statutory provisions do not require DHS or DOJ to adjust penalty amounts based on mitigating circumstances, financial capacity, or subjective good-faith factors, nor do they provide a means for aliens to request discretionary mitigation. In contrast, several of the statutes cited by commenters, including those governing certain employer sanctions under the IRCA, explicitly establish mitigating factors that DHS or DOJ must consider when assessing penalties. Congress included no such language in the statutory provisions at issue here. Additionally, President Trump’s Executive orders direct DHS to “ensure the assessment and collection of all fines and penalties [DHS] is authorized by law to assess and collect from aliens unlawfully present in the United States”51 and to “take all appropriate action to secure the borders of our Nation . . . [including by] deterring and preventing the entry of illegal aliens into the United States.”52 At the same time, the IFR does not foreclose DHS from rescinding or reopening decisions on its own initiative, where doing so is necessary to ensure administrative accuracy, correct an error, or address other considerations relevant to sound enforcement. This internal authority is procedural in nature and serves the agency’s interest in consistency, fairness, and error correction. Allowing aliens to file motions to reopen before DHS for the purpose of seeking mitigation, particularly after the supervisory review process, would undermine the Department’s legitimate interests in finality, administrative efficiency, and the timely initiation of the collection process, which are essential to administering a nationwide penalty scheme. The civil monetary penalties addressed in this rule arising under INA 240B(d), 274(D), and 275(b), 8 U.S.C. 1229c(d), 1324d, and 1325(b), do not confer a statutory right on aliens to request reopening of a final civil monetary penalty determination. The Departments therefore decline to create a new regulatory mechanism that would effectively grant aliens a right to reopen final decisions or seek mitigation after supervisory review has concluded.
各部门也不同意评论者提出的、出于公平考虑应采纳与《移民改革与控制法》项下受制裁雇主类似的减轻因素的观点。这种类比是不恰当的。《移民改革与控制法》明确指示政府在评估雇主处罚时考虑某些减轻因素。相比之下,本案所涉及的处罚是基于法定条件,例如未能遵守自愿离境规定或存在先前的驱逐令,这些条件取决于51《第14159号行政命令》,《保护美国人民免受入侵》。第14159号行政命令,《联邦公报》第90卷第8443页(2025年1月20日)。52《第14165号行政命令》,《确保我国边境安全》,《联邦公报》第90卷第8467页(2025年1月20日)。
The Departments also disagree with commenters who argued that fairness requires adopting mitigation factors analogous to those applicable to employers sanctioned under the IRCA. That comparison is misplaced. IRCA explicitly directs the government to consider certain mitigating factors when assessing employer penalties. By contrast, the penalties at issue here are based on statutory conditions, such as failure to comply with voluntary departure or the presence of a prior removal order, that turn on 51 E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion. E.O. 14159, 90 FR 8443 (Jan. 20, 2025). 52 E.O. 14165, Securing Our Borders, 90 FR 8467 (Jan. 20, 2025).
客观的、基于记录的事实。这些条款并不要求进行自由裁量的多因素处罚评估。各部不需要将国会在其他条文中选择纳入但在此处省略的减轻处罚框架强行引入这些法规之中。基于上述理由,各部得出结论:临时最终规则适当地反映了《移民与国籍法》第240B(d)条、第274D条和第275(b)条(《美国法典》第8编第1229c(d)条、第1324d条和第1325(b)条)项下关于民事处罚的法定框架,采用减轻处罚机制、分级罚款制度、经济状况审查结构或由被申请人提起的重开动议并非适用法规所要求,且在行政上不可行,超出本次规则制定的范围。
objective, records-based facts. These provisions do not require a discretionary, multi- factor penalty assessment. The Departments are not required to import into these statutes mitigation frameworks that Congress has chosen to include elsewhere but omitted here. For these reasons, the Departments conclude that the IFR appropriately reflects the statutory framework governing civil penalties under INA 240B(d), 274D, and 275(b), 8 U.S.C. 1229c(d), 1324d, and 1325(b), and that adopting a mitigation regime, a tiered fine system, a means-tested structure, or respondent-initiated motions to reopen is not required by the governing statutes, and is administratively impractical and beyond the scope of this rulemaking.
8. 民事罚款数额;8 CFR 281.1(g)条 评论:部分评论者认为,临时最终规则所施加的罚款涉及第八修正案的过度罚款条款。他们认为,根据美国最高法院的判例,即使某项处罚被认定为“民事”性质,若其服务于惩罚性或威慑性目的而非补救性目的,则就过度罚款条款而言,该处罚仍构成惩罚。他们普遍声称,临时最终规则授权国土安全部(DHS)发布的罚款不具有任何补救目的,且临时最终规则自述的意图,即威慑未来的非法入境行为并鼓励更广泛地遵守驱逐出境令和自愿离境令,反映出其具有惩罚性目的。评论者普遍表示,这些处罚在第八修正案之下属于过度罚款,因为其可能导致与相关移民违规行为“不成比例”的金额。
8. Civil Penalty Amounts; 8 CFR 281.1(g) Comment: Some commenters argued that the fines imposed by the IFR implicate the Eighth Amendment’s Excessive Fines Clause. They contend that, under U.S. Supreme Court precedent, even if a penalty is deemed “civil,” it constitutes punishment for purposes of the Excessive Fines Clause if it serves retributive or deterrent purposes, rather than remedial ones. They broadly claimed that the IFR authorizes DHS to issue fines that serve no remedial purpose, and the IFR’s self-expressed intent—to deter future unlawful entries and encourage greater compliance with removal and voluntary departure orders—reflects a punitive purpose. Commenters broadly stated that these penalties are excessive under the Eighth Amendment because they can result in sums “disproportionate” to the underlying immigration violations.
一位评论者指出,外国人决定前来美国或在驱逐出境令或自愿离境令下仍滞留在美国境内的情形多种多样,并认为各部未能考虑这些情形违反了过度罚款条款。该评论者列举了若干示例,包括逃离迫害的外国人或为子女寻求更好生活的外国人、因疾病或经济困难而无法离境的外国人、对驱逐出境令提出未决异议的外国人,以及被缺席驱逐出境且对该命令并不知情的外国人。一位评论者建议,国土安全部应根据违规行为的性质及外国人的支付能力,对罚款设定上限。
One commenter noted that there is a wide range of circumstances surrounding an alien’s decision to come to the United States or remain in the country after a removal order or voluntary departure order and suggested that the Departments’ failure to consider these circumstances violates the Excessive Fines Clause. The commenter listed examples, including aliens fleeing persecution or seeking a better life for their children, aliens who were unable to depart due to illness, financial problems, aliens with pending challenges to a removal order, and aliens who were removed in absentia and were unaware of the order. One commenter suggested that DHS should cap fines at levels that reflect the nature of the violation and an alien’s ability to pay.
回应:首先,各部指出,临时最终规则并未改变国会所设定的民事罚款的范围或数额。其仅改变了签发和裁决此类民事罚款以及允许外国人对其提出异议的程序。参见《联邦公报》第90卷第27454页。无论是在临时最终规则所规定的简化处罚程序下,还是在此前8 CFR第280部分所规定的处罚程序下,国土安全部均施加了法定最高处罚数额。这表明,评论者关于第八修正案的担忧主要针对的是国会所授权的处罚数额,而非临时最终规则的程序,因此超出本规则的范围。然而,就评论者提出的临时最终规则因未要求国土安全部移民官员在施加最高处罚数额之前评估相称性或考虑减轻情节而属于违法的观点,各部作出如下回应:(1)这些民事罚款不属于第八修正案过度罚款条款的适用范围;且(2)即便将其视为“罚款”,根据美国诉巴贾卡伊安案(United States v. Bajakajian, 524 U.S. 321 (1998))所确立的因素,其亦不构成过度。
Response: As an initial matter, the Departments note that the IFR did not change the scope or amount of the civil monetary penalties that Congress has established. It only changed the procedures for issuing and adjudicating these civil monetary penalties and for allowing an alien to contest them. See 90 FR 27454. DHS has imposed the maximum statutory penalties under both the IFR’s streamlined penalty process and the prior penalty process in 8 CFR part 280. This indicates that commenters’ Eighth Amendment concerns are directed primarily at the penalty amounts authorized by Congress, not at the IFR’s procedures, and are therefore outside of the scope of this rule. Nevertheless, in so far as commenters are suggesting the IFR is unlawful because it does not require DHS immigration officers to assess proportionality or consider mitigating factors before imposing the maximum penalty amount, the Departments respond as follows: (1) these civil monetary penalties do not fall within the Eighth Amendments Excessive Fines Clause, and (2) even if treated as “fines,” they are not excessive under the factors in United States v. Bajakajian, 524 U.S. 321 (1998).
最高法院从未认定移民违法行为的民事罚款属于《宪法》“过度罚款条款”所称的“罚款”。该领域的判例要么涉及作为刑事犯罪行为处罚手段而判处的制裁,要么涉及在刑事定罪后对用于实施犯罪的财产所采取的民事没收行动。参见Alexander诉United States案,509 U.S. 544, 558(1993)(刑事没收财产);Bajakajian案,524 U.S. at 328(刑事没收货币);Austin诉United States案,509 U.S. 602, 619至622(1993)(对用于实施毒品犯罪的财产的民事没收)。相比之下,本规则所涉及的处罚并非与刑事犯罪相关联而实施。这些是旨在促进遵守移民法律并支持国土安全部(DHS)执法工作的民事措施。最高法院长期以来一直承认,国会可以针对违反国家移民法律的行为施加民事罚款,且此类罚款在性质上并非刑事性质。参见例如Oceanic Steam Nav. Co.诉Stranahan案,214 U.S. 320, 337(1909)。此外,这些罚款旨在发挥威慑作用这一事实,并不使其受制于“过度罚款条款”,因为“威慑作用既可服务于民事目的,也可服务于刑事目的”。参见Hudson诉United States案,522 U.S. 93, 105(1997)(内部引号略去);另见Garner诉美国劳工部案,221 F.3d 822, 827(第五巡回法院, 2000年)(“一项法律具有威慑未来行为的作用,并不当然使其具有惩罚性。”)。此外,国会显然意图使这些罚款具有补救性目的。正如本序言第二部分所解释的那样,逮捕、拘留和驱逐可驱逐外国人,以及开展第240条驱逐程序,给两部门带来了巨大成本,大量非法入境行为也使边境资源承受压力。国会认识到这些危害,并授权征收民事罚款,既鼓励遵守移民法律,又帮助资助国土安全部的边境安全和驱逐工作。参见《移民与国籍法》第280条(b)(3)款,8 U.S.C. 1330(b)(3)(规定移民执法账户可用于驱逐犯罪外国人以及在美国边境某些地段修建相关设施);另见众议院第104至469号报告第一部分,第155页(1996年)。
The Supreme Court has never held that civil monetary penalties for immigration violations are “fines” for purposes of the Excessive Fines Clause. The Court’s cases in this area have either involved sanctions ordered as penalties for criminal conduct, or civil forfeiture actions imposed after a criminal conviction, where the property was used in the commission of the crime. See Alexander v. United States, 509 U.S. 544, 558 (1993) (criminal forfeiture of property); Bajakajian, 524 U.S. at 328 (criminal forfeiture of currency); Austin v. United States, 509 U.S. 602, 619–22 (1993) (civil forfeiture of property used to commit drug crimes). By contrast, the penalties covered by this rule are not imposed in connection with criminal offenses. They are civil measures designed to promote compliance with the immigration laws and support DHS’s enforcement efforts. The Supreme Court has long recognized that Congress may impose civil monetary penalties for violations of the Nation’s immigration laws, and that such penalties are not criminal in nature. See, e.g., Oceanic Steam Nav. Co. v. Stranahan, 214 U.S. 320, 337 (1909). Moreover, the fact that these penalties are intended to serve a deterrence purpose does not render them subject to the Excessive Fines Clause because “deterrence may serve civil as well as criminal goals.” Hudson v. United States, 522 U.S. 93 105, (1997) (internal quotation marks omitted); see also Garner v. U.S. Dep’t of Labor, 221 F.3d 822, 827 (5th Cir. 2000) (“That a statute serves to deter future conduct does not automatically render it punitive.”). In addition, Congress clearly intended these penalties to serve a remedial purpose. As explained in Section II of this preamble, apprehending, detaining, and removing removable aliens, and conducting section 240 removal proceedings imposes significant costs on the Departments, and a large number of unlawful entries strain resources at the border. Congress recognized these harms and authorized civil penalties to both encourage compliance with the immigration laws and to help fund DHS’s border security and removal efforts. See INA 280(b)(3), 8 U.S.C. 1330(b)(3) (providing that the Immigration Enforcement Account is available for removal of criminal aliens and structures along certain portions of the United States border); see also H. Rep. No. 104–469 pt. 1 at 155 (1996).
即使这些罚款被视为“过度罚款条款”所称的罚款,国土安全部在未考虑每位外国人非法入境或在被下达驱逐令或离境令后未能离境的具体个人原因的情况下,决定施加国会授权的最高额度罚款,既不属于过度,也不违宪。各巡回上诉法院通常采用Bajakajian案,524 U.S. at 337至340中确立的以下因素,以判断某项罚款是否与违法行为的严重程度“严重不成比例”:(1)国会授权的罚款数额以及违法行为所造成损害的性质;(2)该法规主要适用的人群类别;(3)与可能适用的刑事处罚进行比较。综合这些因素可以得出结论,无需修订临时最终规则(IFR)以限制国土安全部施加法定最高罚款的权限,也无需在作出民事罚款决定前进行比例性分析,或另行考虑减轻情节因素。
Even if these penalties were considered fines under the Excessive Fines Clause, DHS’s decision to impose the maximum amount authorized by Congress without considering each alien’s individual reasons for unlawful entry or failure to depart after a removal or departure order is neither excessive nor unconstitutional. Courts of appeals generally apply the following factors from Bajakajian, 524 U.S. at 337–40, to determine whether a fine is “grossly disproportional” to the gravity of an offense: (1) the amount of the penalty authorized by Congress and the nature of the harm that results from a violation; (2) the class of persons to whom the statute principally applies; and (3) a comparison with potential criminal penalties. Taken together, these factors support the conclusion that the IFR need not be amended to limit DHS’s authority to impose the maximum statutory penalties or to require a proportionality analysis before issuing a civil penalty or otherwise consider mitigating factors.
关于第一项因素,对违法行为适当处罚金额的判断“首先属于立法机关的职权范围”。Bajakajian案,524 U.S. 第322页。本规则所涉及的民事罚款金额体现了国会的审慎判断,即所涉行为,即外国人非法入境或在被驱逐令或离境令后未能离境,属于特别严重的行为,需要处以巨额罚款以促进对移民法律的遵守。见90 FR 27454。与国会的判断及当前执法重点保持一致,美国国土安全部(DHS)已确定,对近年来非法进入美国或违反驱逐令及离境令的大量外国人处以法定最高罚款是适当的应对措施。见本序言第二节。法院已裁定,只要行政机关的民事处罚决定不超过法定最高限额,该处罚即不构成对第八修正案的过度处罚。见Newell Recycling Co. v. EPA案,231 F.3d 204, 210(第五巡回上诉法院,2000年)(“无论行政罚款在外行人看来显得多么过高,只要该罚款未超出授权该罚款的法规所规定的限额,该罚款即不违反第八修正案”);另见Pharaon v. Bd. of Governors of the Fed. Res. Sys案,135 F.3d 148, 155-57页(哥伦比亚特区巡回上诉法院,1998年)。
With respect to the first factor, judgments about the appropriate penalty amount for a violation “belong in the first instance to the legislature.” Bajakajian, 524 U.S. at 322. The civil monetary penalties covered by this rule reflect Congress’s considered judgment that the conduct involved—an alien’s unlawful entry or failure to depart after a removal or departure order—is particularly serious and warrants substantial fines to promote compliance with immigration laws. See 90 FR 27454. Consistent with Congress’s judgment and current enforcement priorities, DHS has determined that imposing the maximum authorized penalties is an appropriate response to the large number of aliens who have unlawfully entered the United States or have violated removal and departure orders in recent years. See Section II of this preamble. Courts have held that an agency’s civil penalty decision is not excessive under the Eighth Amendment when it does not exceed the statutory maximum. See Newell Recycling Co. v. EPA, 231 F.3d 204, 210 (5th Cir. 2000) (“No matter how excessive (in lay terms) an administrative fine may appear, if the fine does not exceed the limits prescribed by the statute authorizing it, the fine does not violate the Eighth Amendment”); see also Pharaon v. Bd. of Governors of the Fed. Res. Sys, 135 F.3d 148, 155–57 (D.C. Cir. 1998).
法定授权的具体处罚金额与相关移民法律违法行为也是相称的。对于非法入境处罚,经通货膨胀调整后的最高处罚金额为每次入境500美元。见8 CFR 280.53(b)(15)。鉴于国会在制定该处罚时所关切的问题,该金额对于违反移民法律的非法入境行为,包括多次非法入境行为,并非不成比例。见众议院第104-469号报告第155页(1996年)(指出非法入境“如果不承担后果,将削弱我们确保边境安全努力的公信力”)。对于自愿离境民事罚款,国会已授权移民法官在经通货膨胀调整后的1,992美元至9,970美元之间设定处罚金额。见8 CFR 280.53(b)(3)。根据长期施行的法规,移民法官或移民上诉委员会(BIA)必须在该范围内选择一个能够反映该外国人遵守自愿离境可能性的金额,或可采用3,000美元的推定金额。见8 CFR 1240.26(l);73 FR 76927, 76936页(2008年12月18日)。DHS遵循行政审判与上诉办公室(EOIR)所设定的金额。该金额并非过高,因为它既反映了裁决者对确保遵守所需激励程度的评估,也体现了国会希望确保违反离境令行为须承担后果的意愿。见众议院第104-469号报告第122页(指出在《非法移民改革和移民责任法》(IIRIRA)颁布之前,获准自愿离境的外国人“几乎没有什么动力”真正离开美国)。
The specific statutorily authorized penalty amounts are also proportionate to the underlying immigration law violations. For the unlawful entry penalty, the maximum penalty (adjusted for inflation) is $500 for each entry. See 8 CFR 280.53(b)(15). That amount is not disproportionate to unlawful entries, including repeated unlawful entries, in violation of the immigration laws in light of Congress’s concerns when enacting this penalty. See H. Rep. No. 104–469 at 155 (1996) (noting that unlawful entry “with no consequences undermines the credibility of our efforts to secure the border”). For voluntary departure civil penalties, Congress has authorized Immigration Judges to set penalties between $1,992 and $9,970 (as adjusted for inflation). See 8 CFR 280.53(b)(3). Under longstanding regulations, Immigration Judges or the BIA must select an amount within that range that reflects the likelihood that the alien will comply with voluntary departure or may apply a presumptive amount of $3,000. See 8 CFR 1240.26(l); 73 FR 76927, 76936 (Dec. 18, 2008). DHS adheres to the amount set by EOIR. That amount is not excessive because it reflects both the adjudicator’s assessment of the incentive needed to secure compliance and Congress’s desire to ensure consequences for violating departure orders. See H. Rep. No. 104–469, at 122 (noting how aliens with voluntary departure and had “very little incentive” to actually depart the United States prior to IIRIRA).
对于在驱逐令下未能离境的情形,国会已授权DHS对外国人故意未能离境的每一天处以经通货膨胀调整后每天998美元的罚款。有评论者认为,这些处罚过高,因为在某些情况下累计总额可能超过1,000,000美元。两部门不认同此观点。国会设计该条款的本意是,故意未能离境的每一天均构成一项单独的、重复发生的违法行为。见本序言第三节E部分。在此背景下,相称性应以每日处罚金额来评估,而非以累计总额来评估。例如参见United States v. Schwarzbaum案,127 F.4th 259, 276页(第十一巡回上诉法院,2025年)。与其他移民相关的民事处罚相比,每日998美元的处罚并不过高。该金额体现了国会对外国人每多滞留美国一天、故意违反驱逐令所造成危害严重性的判断,以及此类故意违法行为如何削弱移民法律公信力,从而需要单独处罚的考量。
For failure to depart after a removal order, Congress has authorized DHS to impose a penalty of $998 per day (as adjusted for inflation) for each day an alien willfully fails to depart. Commenters argue that these penalties are excessive because the cumulative total may, in some cases, exceed $1,000,000. The Departments disagree. Congress designed this provision so that each day of willful failure to depart constitutes a separate, repeating violation. See Section III.E. of this preamble. In this context, proportionality is assessed by looking at each per day penalty, not the cumulative amount. See e.g. United States v. Schwarzbaum, 127 F.4th 259, 276 (11th Cir. 2025). A $998 per diem penalty is not excessive compared to other immigration-related civil penalties. It reflects Congress’s judgment as to the seriousness of the harm that flows from each day an alien remains in the United States in willful violation of a removal order and how these willful violations undermine the credibility of the immigration laws and warrant a separate penalty.
即使累积计算,逾期不离境的按日罚款也并非过高。国会在《1996年非法移民改革和移民责任法》(IIRIRA)中增加了这一权力,以加强移民执法并加重对无视驱逐令行为的后果,这是针对外国人在最终驱逐令作出后仍滞留美国(有时长达数年)这一问题而作出的回应。参见众议院报告第104-469号,第1部分,第160页(1996年)(该报告在解释制定IIRIRA的目的时指出,“持有最终驱逐令的外国人[往往]无限期滞留美国”,并指出“因此,用于识别、拘捕和为可驱逐外国人提供听证的资源往往被浪费”)。在此背景下,国会明确授权美国国土安全部(DHS)对外国人故意长期违反驱逐令滞留美国的行为,施加可能达到巨额总数的按日罚款。在这种情况下,即使适用每日最高罚款金额导致总额巨大,也并非过高,因为该总额直接反映了持续且反复的违法行为。参见Schwarzbaum案,127 F.4th 284(“当累积罚款由若干符合宪法比例原则的单项罚款构成时,该累积罚款‘恰恰是与违法行为直接成比例的’”)(引用及内部引号省略)。
Even when viewed cumulatively, the per diem failure to depart penalty is not excessive. Congress added this authority in IIRIRA to strengthen immigration enforcement and to increase the consequences for ignoring removal orders, in response to the problem of aliens remaining in the United States—sometimes for years—after final deportation orders. See H.R. Rep. No. 104–469, pt. 1, at 160 (1996) (explaining when enacting IIRIRA that “aliens who have final orders of deportation [often] remain in the United States indefinitely” and noting that “[a]s a result, the resources expended to identify, apprehend, and provide a hearing to a deportable alien are all too often wasted”). Against this backdrop, Congress expressly authorized DHS to impose per-day penalties that may reach substantial sums when an alien willfully remains in the United States for extended periods in violation of a removal order. In these circumstances, imposing the maximum daily amount, even where it results in a large total, is not excessive because the total directly reflects a long‑running, repeated violation. See Schwarzbaum, 127 F.4th at 284 (“when a cumulative fine is made up of constitutionally proportionate penalties, the cumulative fine “is, literally, directly proportionate to the offense”) (citation and internal quotations omitted).
综上所述,本规则所涉及的三类罚款体现了国会对每种违法行为所造成损害的审慎评估。针对非法入境的较低罚款通常适用于在入境时被拘捕的外国人,这些人往往可被迅速遣返或适用快速遣返程序,从而节省了与第240条驱逐程序相关的资源。针对逾期不离境的较高罚款则体现了国会的判断,即在离境令或驱逐令作出后仍滞留美国的行为,不仅严重损害移民制度的完整性,还造成执法和裁决资源的浪费。参见众议院报告第104-469号,第1部分,第160页。因此,相关部门认为,对这些罚款适用法定最高金额,既符合国会制定这些罚款的立法目的,也并非过高,因为该金额反映了国会对此类违法行为所认定的损害价值。参见United States v. Chaplin’s, Inc.案,646 F.3d 846,852(第十一巡回上诉法院,2011年)(“国会作为代议机构,可以提炼出社会对有害行为所赋予的货币价值”)。
Taken together, the three penalties covered by this rule reflect Congress’s calibrated assessment of the harm associated with each type of violation. Lower penalties for unlawful entry generally apply to aliens apprehended while entering and who are often subject to prompt return or expedited removal, thereby conserving resources associated with section 240 removal proceedings. Higher penalties for failure to depart reflect Congress’s judgment that remaining in the United States after a departure or removal order both seriously undermines the integrity of the immigration system and results in wasted enforcement and adjudicative resources. See H. Rep. No. 104 469, pt. 1, at 160. Accordingly, the Departments believe that imposing the maximum statutorily authorized amount of these penalties is both consistent with Congress’s purpose in enacting these penalties and not excessive given those amounts reflect the value of the harm Congress placed on such violations. See United States v. Chaplin’s, Inc., 646 F.3d 846, 852 (11th Cir. 2011) (“Congress, as a representative body, can distill the monetary value society places on harmful conduct”).
Bajakajian案确立的第二项因素,即罚款是否适用于该法规主要针对的人群类别,也支持相关部门的立场。受这些罚款约束的外国人完全属于国会所针对的类别。对于非法入境罚款而言,外国人在试图非法入境美国时被拘捕这一事实本身即足以支持处以罚款。对于逾期不离境罚款而言,美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)的指导意见要求执法人员确认该外国人确属最终驱逐令或自愿离境令的对象、DHS记录中没有其已离境的记录,且根据现有记录,不存在可排除其故意或自愿不离境的法律障碍或其他情形。对于按日罚款而言,ICE的指导意见进一步要求执法人员将该外国人依法获准在美国停留的期间(例如驱逐令被暂缓执行期间)予以排除。这正是国会所要求的全部内容。国会已明确规定,任何实施被禁止行为,即非法入境美国,或在驱逐令或离境令作出后故意或自愿不离境的外国人,均须承担最高可达法定上限的民事罚款。与其他移民相关的民事罚款不同53,也与评论者的建议相反,国会并未要求DHS根据比例原则调整罚款金额,或考虑具体的减轻情节。若评论者意指DHS必须考虑诸如外国人无法获得旅行证件、住院治疗或其他53 参见,例如,《移民与国籍法》第274A(e)(5)条,8 U.S.C. 1324a(e)(5)(列明在确定某些与就业相关的移民违法行为适当罚款金额时必须考虑的各项因素);《移民与国籍法》第273(e)条,8 U.S.C. 1323(e)(指示DHS制定法规,在适当情况下减轻承运人运送无证件外国人的罚款)。
The second Bajakajian factor—whether the penalties apply to the class of persons for whom the statute was principally designed—also supports the Departments’ position. Aliens subject to these penalties fall squarely within the class Congress targeted. For unlawful entry penalties, an alien’s apprehension while attempting to enter the United States unlawfully is sufficient to support the penalty. For failure to depart penalties, ICE guidance instructs officers to confirm that the alien is subject to a final removal or voluntary departure order, that there is no DHS record of the alien’s departure, and that, based on available records, there are no legal impediments or other circumstances that would negate a willful or voluntary failure to depart. For per diem penalties, ICE guidance further requires officers to exclude periods when the alien was lawfully permitted to remain in the United States, such as during the pendency of a stay of removal. This is all Congress has required. Congress has clearly provided that any alien who engages in the proscribed conduct—unlawfully entering the United States or willfully or voluntarily failing to depart after a removal or departure order—is subject to a civil penalty up to the statutory maximum. By contrast to other immigration-related civil penalties53 and contrary to commenters’ suggestion, Congress has not required DHS to adjust the penalty based on proportionality or to consider specific mitigating circumstances. To the extent that commenters are suggesting that DHS must consider factors such as an alien’s inability to obtain travel documents, hospitalization, or other 53 See e.g., INA 274A(e)(5), 8 U.S.C. 1324a(e)(5) (listing various factors that must be considered when determining the appropriate amount of the penalty for certain employment-related immigration violations); INA 273(e), 8 U.S.C. 1323(e) (directing DHS to prescribe regulations for reducing carrier penalties for transporting undocumented aliens in appropriate circumstances).
由于外国人无法控制的情况,这些因素可能会否定外国人行为的“故意性”或“自愿性”,如果外国人在上诉中证明这些情况,该外国人可能根本不应受到处罚。然而,外国人最适合在上诉中提出这些考虑因素,因为国土安全部(DHS)通常不会了解此类情况。因此,第二个Bajakajian因素支持国土安全部在不事先进行相称性分析或考虑减轻情节的情况下,按法定授权的最高金额评估这些处罚的决定。第三个Bajakajian因素,即与潜在刑事制裁的比较,同样不利于认定过度性的成立。在《1996年非法移民改革和移民责任法案》(IIRIRA)中,国会不仅设立了未离境民事处罚,还修订了《移民与国籍法》(INA)第243条(美国法典第8编第1253节),将外国人在最终驱逐令发布后90天内故意不离境或拒绝离境的行为定为刑事犯罪。国会授权对此处以最高四年监禁、罚款或两者并处,对某些刑事外国人适用加重处罚。见INA第243(a)(1)条,美国法典第8编第1253(a)(1)节。国会还授权对非法入境行为处以刑事处罚,包括最高六个月监禁、罚款或两者并处,对再犯者加重处罚。见INA第275(a)条,美国法典第8编第1325(a)节。这些刑事处罚远比本案所涉的民事处罚严厉,而国会特别规定,除任何刑事制裁外,还可额外施加最高额的民事处罚。见INA第274D(b)条、第275(b)条,美国法典第8编第1324d(b)节、第1325(b)节。鉴于此项法定框架,所有三个Bajakajian因素都强烈不利于评论者关于施加最高法定民事处罚违反《过度罚款条款》的论点。
circumstances beyond the alien’s control, such factors could negate the “willfulness” or “voluntariness” of an alien’s conduct and, if the alien demonstrates them on appeal, the alien may not be subject to the penalty at all. However, the alien is best positioned to raise these considerations on appeal because DHS would generally not have knowledge of such circumstances. The second Bajakajian factor therefore supports DHS’s decision to assess these penalties up to the maximum statutorily authorized amount without first conducting a proportionality analysis or considering mitigating circumstances. The third Bajakajian factor—comparison to potential criminal sanctions— likewise weighs against a finding of excessiveness. In IIRIRA, Congress not only created the civil failure to depart penalty but also amended INA 243, 8 U.S.C. 1253, to make it a criminal offense for an alien to willfully fail or refuse to depart within 90 days of a final removal order. Congress authorized imprisonment of up to four years, a fine, or both, with enhanced penalties for certain criminal aliens. See INA 243(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1253(a)(1). Congress has also authorized criminal penalties for unlawful entry, including up to six months’ imprisonment, a fine, or both, with increased penalties for repeat offenses. See INA 275(a), 8 U.S.C. 1325(a). These criminal penalties are significantly more severe than the civil penalties at issue, and Congress specifically provided that the maximum civil penalties may be imposed in addition to any criminal sanctions. See INA 274D(b), 275(b), 8 U.S.C. 1324d(b), 1325(b). In light of this statutory framework, all three Bajakajian factors weigh strongly against commenters’ arguments that imposing the maximum statutory civil penalties violates the Excessive Fines Clause.
基于上述原因,各部不同意修改本规则以限制国土安全部移民官员酌情施加最高法定处罚金额的权力,也不要求其在施加处罚前适用相称性因素。最后,关于一位评论者对“支付能力”的担忧,各部指出,国会并未要求国土安全部在评估这些处罚时考虑外国人的支付能力,最高法院也未裁定《过度罚款条款》必须分析此项因素。在没有法律要求国土安全部考虑外国人支付能力的情况下,国土安全部认为,根据外国人的支付能力下调罚款金额将削弱本规则的威慑效果。
For these reasons, the Departments decline to amend the rule to limit the discretion of DHS immigration officers to impose the maximum statutory penalty amounts or require that they apply proportionality factors before doing so. Finally, regarding one commenter’s concern about “ability to pay,” the Departments note that Congress has not required DHS to consider an alien’s ability to pay in assessing these penalties, and the Supreme Court has not held this factor must be analyzed under the Excessive Fines Clause. Absent a legal requirement for DHS to consider an alien’s ability to pay, DHS believes that adjusting a fine amount lower based on an alien’s ability to pay would diminish the rule’s deterrent effect.
评论:一位评论者声称,8 CFR 281.1(g)与8 CFR 1240.26(l)不一致,后者要求移民法官或移民上诉委员会(BIA)在准予自愿离境时告知外国人民事罚款金额,否则将设定3,000美元的推定金额。另一位评论者指出,国土安全部(DHS)已随时间推移逐步提高8 CFR 280.53中关于未能离境违规行为的罚款金额,从2011年的每日500美元逐步提高到2025年的每日998美元,DHS在此期间每年都进行递增调整。该评论者声称,“在任何特定日期据称累积的罚款金额应限于该日期有效法规所授权的金额”。该评论者承认《2015年联邦民事罚款通货膨胀调整改进法》允许DHS每年对民事罚款金额进行通货膨胀调整并可追溯适用调整,但该评论者认为,“在未逐项列明DHS声称在具体日期累积的确切金额,也未解释DHS为何认为罚款在该特定时间段内累积的情况下,径行收取一笔总额,使被申请人被剥夺了对此类指控提出异议所必需的关键信息。”该评论者认为,临时最终规则(IFR)存在缺陷,因为它未要求DHS逐项列明“DHS声称在具体日期累积的”罚款,“也未提供DHS为何认为罚款在该特定时间段内累积的任何解释”。 回应:首先,DHS指出,该IFR并未指示移民官员适用8 CFR 280.53所列的罚款金额。相反,8 CFR 281.1(g)仅是将移民官员引导至该条款,以便他们能够获取有关各项罚款当前经通货膨胀调整后金额的信息。移民官员必须根据美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)和美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)的指南,就如何在每个个案中适用这些经通货膨胀调整的费率,或是否适用这些费率作出判断。事实上,与一位评论者的说法相反,ICE的指南指示移民官员在评估自愿离境民事罚款时,适用移民法官或BIA设定的罚款金额,若自愿离境令中未包含罚款金额,则适用3,000美元的金额。因此,DHS评估自愿离境罚款的方式符合8 CFR 1240.26(l)的规定。
Comment: One commenter alleged that 8 CFR 281.1(g) is inconsistent with 8 CFR 1240.26(l) mandating that the Immigration Judge or BIA advise the alien of the civil penalty amount at the time of granting voluntary departure or a presumptive amount of $3,000 will be set. Another commenter noted that DHS has increased the penalty amounts in 8 CFR 280.53 for failure to depart violations incrementally over time from $500 per diem penalty in 2011, to a $998 per diem penalty in 2025, with DHS making incremental adjustments each year in between. The commenter claims that “the amount of fines that allegedly accrued on any given day should have been limited to the amount authorized by the regulation in effect on that day.” The commenter acknowledges that the Federal Civil Penalties Inflation Adjustment Improvements Act of 2015 allows DHS to adjust civil penalty amounts for inflation each year and to apply adjustments retroactively, but the commenter argues that “charging a lump sum without itemizing the exact amounts that DHS alleges to have accrued on any specific dates nor any explanation for why DHS believes fines accrued during this particular timeframe deprives respondents of critical information necessary to contest such charges.” The commenter argues the IFR is defective because it does not require DHS to itemize penalties that “DHS alleges to have accrued on any specific dates nor [provides] any explanation for why DHS believes fines accrued during this particular timeframe.” Response: First, DHS notes that the IFR does not direct immigration officers to apply the penalty amounts listed in 8 CFR 280.53. Rather, 8 CFR 281.1(g) merely refers them to that provision so that they have access to information concerning the current inflation adjusted amount for each penalty. Immigration officers must determine how to apply those inflation adjusted rates in the context of each individual case, or whether to apply those rates at all, consistent with ICE and CBP guidance. Indeed, contrary to one commenter’s claim, ICE guidance directs immigration officers to apply the penalty amount set by an Immigration Judge or the BIA or a $3,000 amount if no penalty amount is included in the voluntary departure order when assessing voluntary departure civil penalties. Accordingly, DHS is assessing voluntary departure penalties consistent with 8 CFR 1240.26(l).
此外,各部门不同意DHS应在依据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第274D(a)(1)条、8 U.S.C. 1324d(a)(1)对故意未能离境施加罚款的处罚决定中逐项列明罚款金额。该评论者提出的“在任何特定日期据称累积的罚款金额应限于该日期有效法规所授权的金额”这一建议,与《2015年联邦民事罚款通货膨胀调整改进法》(公法114-74号,2015年11月2日),28 U.S.C. 2461注释不符。2015年修正案修订了《通货膨胀调整法》第6条,规定“本法下民事罚款金额的任何增加,仅适用于在该增加生效之日后被评估的民事罚款,包括其相关违规行为发生在该增加之前的情形。”国会明确提及将调整适用于“相关违规行为先于”调整生效日期的民事罚款,这清楚地表明,2015年后的通货膨胀调整可适用于在提高后的民事罚款金额生效日期之前发生的违规行为,但前提是该民事罚款是在“增加生效之日后被评估的”。这正是DHS适用INA第274D(a)(1)条、8 U.S.C. 1324d(a)(1)规定的未能离境罚款的方式。具体而言,DHS适用的是罚款被“评估”之日,即DHS作出处罚决定之日,当时有效的经通货膨胀调整后的金额,而不论遣返令的日期或该罚款所适用期间内任何一天所授权的金额为何,即外国人在最终遣返令作出后故意未能离开美国的任何一天。
Further, the Departments do not agree that DHS should itemize penalty amounts in penalty decisions imposing fines for willful failure to depart under INA 274D(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1324d(a)(1). The commenter’s suggestion that “the amount of fines that allegedly accrued on any given day should [be] limited to the amount authorized by the regulation in effect on that day” is inconsistent with the Federal Civil Penalties Inflation Adjustment Improvements Act of 2015, Public Law 114–74 (Nov. 2, 2015), 28 U.S.C. 2461 note. The 2015 Amendments amended section 6 of the Inflation Adjustment Act to provide that “[a]ny increase under this Act in a civil monetary penalty shall apply only to civil monetary penalties, including those whose associated violation predated such increase, which are assessed after the date the increase takes effect.” Congress’s specific reference to applying the adjustments to civil monetary penalties “whose associated violation predated” the effective date of the adjustment clearly contemplates that inflation adjustments after 2015 can be applied to violations occurring prior to the effective date of the increased civil penalty amounts—but only if the civil penalties are “assessed after the date the increase takes effect.” That is precisely how DHS applies the failure to depart penalties under INA 274D(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1324d(a)(1). Specifically, DHS applies the inflation adjusted amount that is in effect on the day that the penalty is “assessed”—i.e., when DHS issues the penalty decision—regardless of the date of the removal order or the amount that was authorized on any one day in the period for which the penalty applies— i.e., any day that the alien willfully failed to depart the United States after the final removal order.
就评论者担忧国土安全部(DHS)将现行通货膨胀调整费率适用于2015年《通货膨胀调整改进法》生效之前发生的违法行为一事,国土安全部指出该评论者的担忧并无依据。如本序言前文所述,外国人须就其在最终驱逐令下达后故意滞留美国的每一天承担按日计罚,无论该驱逐令最初何时成为最终令。然而,国土安全部仅就处罚决定作出之日起向前追溯五年内的未离境行为适用该按日计罚。因此,即使外国人的驱逐令于2015年之前即已成为最终令,国土安全部也仅会按照处罚决定作出时适用的调整费率,对该决定作出之日起向前追溯五年期间评估按日计罚。
To the extent that the commenter is concerned about DHS applying the current inflation adjusted rate to violations that occurred prior to the Inflation Adjustment Improvements Act of 2015, DHS notes that the commenter’s concern is misplaced. As discussed above in this preamble, an alien is liable for a per diem penalty for each day he willfully remains in the United States after a final removal order regardless of when the removal order first became final. However, DHS is applying the failure to depart penalty for only five years back from the date of the penalty decision. Therefore, DHS would only assess the per diem penalty at the adjusted rate in place at the time of the penalty decision for a total of five years back from the date of the decision, even if the alien’s removal order became final prior to 2015.
关于评论者建议国土安全部应逐项列明处罚决定,因为外国人在其滞留美国期间的某些时段内可能无需承担按日计罚的问题,美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)的指南要求移民官员考虑驱逐令成为行政终局令之后,外国人获授权在美滞留的任何期间(例如,驱逐暂缓或其后获批准的移民福利)。参见本序言第III.E.2节。就外国人个人情况导致其在某段时间内未能故意或自愿离境获得豁免而言(例如,外国人曾住院或被监禁,或因超出其控制范围的情况而无法离境),该外国人最适合在上诉中提出这些问题。不能期望国土安全部了解导致外国人无法离境的个人具体情况。E. 与临时最终规则(IFR)相关的其他事项
Regarding commenters’ suggestion that DHS needs to itemize penalty decisions because the alien may not be liable for a per diem penalty for some portion of the period that the alien remained in the United States, ICE guidance directs immigration officers account for any periods after the removal order became administratively final during which the alien was authorized to remain in the United States (e.g., a stay of removal, a later approved immigration benefit). See Section III.E.2 of this preamble. To the extent that an alien’s individual circumstances excused an alien’s willful or voluntary failure to depart for some period of time (e.g., the alien was hospitalized or incarcerated or otherwise was unable to depart due to circumstances beyond the alien’s control), the alien is best positioned to raise these issues on appeal. DHS cannot be expected to know about an alien’s individual circumstances that prevented the alien from departing. E. Other Matters Related to the IFR
1. 诉讼时效 评论：多位评论者主张,该规则未说明联邦诉讼时效如何适用于临时最终规则(IFR)所规定的民事处罚。一位评论者认为,IFR简化的处罚程序使外国人难以提出诉讼时效抗辩,而这些抗辩的复杂性破坏了IFR关于这些处罚具有易于核实且直截了当性质的假设。一位评论者主张,IFR并不要求国土安全部(DHS)在处罚决定中载明违法行为发生的时间,这使外国人及其律师难以确定DHS是否正确适用了诉讼时效。该评论者认为,15天的上诉期限加上以普通邮件送达决定的做法,可能使外国人及其律师无法就诉讼时效问题进行调查并将诉讼时效作为对这些处罚的抗辩理由提出。该评论者主张,取消移民上诉委员会(BIA)审查尤其成问题,因为DHS移民官员不具备解决复杂诉讼时效问题所需的法律专业知识,而且DHS也没有机制就包括诉讼时效在内的法律问题作出具有约束力的裁决。评论者就联邦诉讼时效法规,即28 U.S.C. 2462,如何适用于本规则所涉处罚发表了各自的看法。关于未按期离境处罚,评论者称,五年诉讼时效期自自愿离境期限届满或驱逐令生效之时起算。他们表示,DHS不能就五年以上之前发生的行为,例如在自愿离境令期限届满后或驱逐令生效后继续滞留美国的行为,施加罚款,尤其是在没有任何判例法或法规承认这些处罚适用“持续违法”理论的情况下。关于非法入境处罚,评论者表示,时效期自外国人在试图进入或正在进入美国过程中被拘留之时起算。 回应：各部不同意应因该规则未包含诉讼时效条款而予以撤销的意见。首先,各部强调IFR的适用范围有限。IFR仅改变了DHS签发和裁决某些民事罚款的程序,以及外国人对其提出异议的方式。IFR并未改变或变更签发这些处罚的标准,包括DHS如何解释或适用适用于这些处罚的一般联邦诉讼时效法规。事实上,各部指出,各部其他民事罚款法规均未包含诉讼时效条款,包括在IFR之前规范本规则所涉处罚评估程序的8 CFR 280。因此,各部认为,鉴于IFR旨在简化未按期离境处罚和非法入境处罚程序这一狭窄目的,将IFR的适用范围与各部其他民事处罚法规的范围保持一致是合理且适当的。因此,各部拒绝修改该规则以增加诉讼时效条款。基于同样的理由,各部不同意评论者所提出的,未包含诉讼时效条款使该规则构成武断或反复无常的意见。[54] 各部同样不同意IFR的简化程序使外国人过于难以提出诉讼时效抗辩的意见。根据8 CFR 281.1(c)(1)的规定,每份处罚决定均列明外国人受最终驱逐令或自愿离境令约束之日期,或外国人因非法进入或试图进入美国而被拘留之日期。参见DHS表格281《违规通知及命令》。因此,凡外国人认为违法行为发生在诉讼时效期之外的,处罚决定本身即包含可用以对处罚提出异议的相关信息。此外,外国人本应已持有用以对处罚决定所载信息提出异议所需的任何文件。例如,[54] 就依IFR签发的任何民事处罚被认定与诉讼时效不符的情形而言,处理此类罚款的问题超出本最终规则制定的范围。最终规则的序言旨在满足《行政程序法》(APA)要求的“关于[该规则]依据和目的的简明概括说明”(5 U.S.C. 553(c)),而处理已依IFR签发的罚款问题,即便确有必要,显然也超出该序言的范畴。
1. Statute of limitations Comment: Several commenters asserted that the rule does not explain how the federal statute of limitations applies to the civil penalties imposed under the IFR. One commenter suggested that the IFR’s streamlined penalty procedures make it difficult for aliens to raise statute of limitations defenses, and the complexity of these defenses undermines the IFR’s assumption about the readily verifiable and straightforward nature of these penalties. One commenter argued that the IFR does not require DHS to include information in the penalty decision about when the violation occurred which makes it difficult for aliens and their counsel to determine if DHS is correctly applying the statute of limitations. The commenter suggested that the 15-day appeal period, combined with the use of ordinary mail to serve decisions, could prevent aliens and their attorneys from investigating statute of limitations issues and raising the statute of limitations as a defense to these penalties. The commenter argued that eliminating BIA review is particularly problematic because DHS immigration officers do not have the legal expertise to resolve complex statute of limitations issues, and DHS does not have a mechanism to issue binding decisions on legal issues including those involving statutes of limitation. Commenters offered their views regarding how the federal statute of limitations, 28 U.S.C. 2462, applies to the penalties covered by this rule. For the failure to depart penalties, commenters claimed that the five-year statute of limitations begins to run the moment the voluntary departure period ends or when a removal order becomes final. They stated that DHS cannot impose fines for conduct that happened more than five years ago, such as remaining in the United States after a voluntary departure order period expired or after a removal order became final, particularly when there is no caselaw or statute recognizing a “continuing violation” theory for these penalties. For the unlawful entry penalties, commenters said that the limitations period begins to run once an alien is apprehended while in the process of entering or attempting to enter the United States. Response: The Departments disagree that the rule should be rescinded because it fails to include a statute of limitations provision. As an initial matter, the Departments emphasize the IFR’s limited scope. The IFR changed only the procedures by which DHS issues and adjudicates certain civil monetary penalties and the manner in which aliens may contest them. The IFR did not change or alter the criteria for issuing these penalties, including how DHS interprets or applies the general federal statute of limitations for these penalties. Indeed, the Departments note that none of the Departments’ other civil monetary penalty regulations contain a statute of limitations provision, including 8 CFR 280, which, prior to the IFR, governed the process for assessing the penalties covered by this rule. Accordingly, the Departments believe that it was reasonable and appropriate to limit the scope of the IFR consistent with the scope of the Departments’ other civil penalty regulations, especially because of the IFR’s narrow purpose of streamlining the process for the failure to depart and unlawful entry penalties. Therefore, the Departments decline to amend the rule to add a statute of limitations provision. For the same reasons, the Departments do not agree with commenters’ suggestion that failure to include a statute of limitations provisions renders the rule arbitrary or capricious.54 The Departments also disagree that the IFR’s streamlined proceedings make it too difficult for aliens to assert a statute of limitations defense. Consistent with 8 CFR 281.1(c)(1), each penalty decision lists the date when the alien became subject to a final removal or a voluntary departure order, or when the alien was apprehended entering or attempting to enter the United States unlawfully. See DHS Form 281, Notice of Violation and Order. Therefore, to the extent that an alien believes that the violation occurred outside of the statute of limitations, the penalty decision itself contains the relevant information to dispute the penalty. Further, aliens should already possess any documents needed to dispute the information contained in the penalty decision. For example, an 54 To the extent that any civil penalties issued under the IFR were determined to be inconsistent with the statute of limitations, addressing such fines would be outside of the scope of this final rulemaking. The preamble to a final rule exists to satisfy the APA’s requirement of “a concise general statement of [the rule’s] basis and purpose,” 5 U.S.C. 553(c), and addressing fines already issued under the IFR—to the extent that is even necessary—plainly falls outside of that.
对最终驱逐令或自愿离境令的日期有异议的外国人,可以直接提供其驱逐令或离境令的副本,或者任何能够证明其驱逐令或自愿离境期限因上诉或驱逐暂缓而延迟的文件。对于因非法入境而被处以民事处罚的外国人,情况亦是如此:相关的指控文件将列明美国国土安全部(DHS)拦截该外国人进入或试图进入美国的日期。评论者并未说明外国人为何无法保留这些重要文件,各部门也不会作此假设。因此,各部门认为,处罚决定书中包含的信息足以使外国人提出诉讼时效抗辩,临时最终规则(IFR)规定的15天上诉期为外国人及其律师(如有)收集证据以补充或准备此类抗辩提供了充分的时间。
alien who disputes the date of a final removal or voluntary departure order can simply provide a copy of his removal or departure order or any documents showing that the alien’s removal order or voluntary departure period was delayed because of an appeal or a stay of removal. The same is true for aliens subject to civil penalties for unlawful entry: the relevant charging document will list the date that DHS apprehended the alien entering or attempting to enter the United States. Commenters do not explain why aliens cannot retain these important documents, and the Departments will not assume otherwise. Accordingly, the Departments believe that the penalty decision contains sufficient information to allow an alien to raise a statute of limitations defense, and the IFR’s 15- day appeal period provides ample time for aliens and their attorneys, if any, to gather evidence to compliment or prepare such a defense.
同样,各部门不同意评论者所持的观点,即需要移民上诉委员会(BIA)进行审查,因为有关诉讼时效的法律问题无法由国土安全部移民官员在上诉中充分解决。
Similarly, the Departments do not agree with commenters’ position that BIA review is needed because legal questions regarding the statute of limitations cannot adequately be resolved by DHS immigration officers on appeal.
美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)和美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)均以直接明了的方式适用这些处罚的诉讼时效,这驳斥了评论者关于因诉讼时效问题复杂而需要包括BIA审查在内的额外程序的论点。首先,ICE仅在外国人的自愿离境期限在处罚决定作出之日起五年内届满的情况下,才根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第240B(d)条,即8 U.S.C. 1229c(d)条评估处罚。55 CBP仅对在过去五年内被拦截进入(或试图进入)美国的外国人,根据INA第275(b)条,即8 U.S.C. 1325(b)条评估处罚。各部门指出,这些做法与评论者对适用于这些处罚的一般诉讼时效规定,即28 U.S.C. 2462条的解释是一致的。这种适用方式确保了这些处罚评估的一致性,55 国土安全部承认,一些罚款是基于自愿离境期限届满五年以上发生的违规行为而开出的,目前正在采取行动,以确保今后开出的罚款符合诉讼时效的规定。
